- **Repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Preserve the required conversation-title convention `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删` and the existing bootstrap/protocol/handoff workflow. Main is the only current branch; no separate checkpoint branch exists.

- **Implementation and regression commits:** `c584d5cad454b8f58b8218d9d109b09f9394ac8f` (fix the Clause 45 CLI parser and error paths); `3c727259a28515e6a18b7bc8e8b4d94e2cfe4477` (add focused parser regression); `91e1d3d47b898ca3649d65c0b5f6e85db1f2bcfa` (extend switch CLI Actions with production-source host compile and sanitizer regression); `dea77de9896bbf4be067029609099b4020c53e3b` (correct workflow path-filter assertions to scope the two checks to the push/pull_request filter block). This is the current code/workflow head before this documentation checkpoint.
- **Recovered exact task:** resumed the previous handoff's `trunk/user/utils/src/switch/mii_mgr_cl45.c` `case 'd'` / `case 'r'` follow-up. Read the actual utility, its Makefile target, the shared `ra_mii_ioctl_data` ABI and the Linux 4.4 RAETH ioctl dispatch.
- **Confirmed defect:** `case 'd'` fell through into `case 'r'`. When `-r` preceded `-d`, the device-address option overwrote `reg_addr`; the same CLI operation could therefore target a different Clause 45 register depending on option order. The existing parser also accepted partial/overflowing/negative numeric values, left missing fields uninitialized, did not require a coherent read/write mode, opened the socket before validating a complete request, and discarded `close()` errors.
- **Fix:** separated `-d` and `-r` handling; added a strict hexadecimal parser with end-pointer/ERANGE checks and field bounds (port 0..31, DEVAD 0..31, register 0..65535, write value 0..65535); zero-initialized ioctl data and interface request; required mode/port/device/register and a write value only for `-s`; rejects conflicting read/write methods, incomplete options and trailing arguments before socket creation; preserves the legacy `-t` no-op and successful no-argument/`-?` help; reports socket/ioctl failures as process failure and reports close failure after an otherwise successful ioctl without masking an earlier failure.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/utils/src/switch/mii_mgr_cl45.c`; new `trunk/user/utils/src/switch/tests/mii_mgr_cl45_cli.c`; `.github/workflows/switch-cli-stability.yml`.
- **Host validation:** GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` production-source object compile PASS on a local source copy; GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` ASan/UBSan + leak-detection parser regression PASS (`mii_mgr_cl45 argument parsing, ordering and range checks: PASS`). The local container did not have a repository checkout; the local check used a minimal userspace RA ioctl header matching the repository's `ra_mii_ioctl_data` field layout. The Actions workflow now compiles the checked-in production source against the actual repository RAETH header and executes the test under ASan/UBSan, but that pushed run's result has not yet been retrieved.
- **Exact-head CI state:** workflow triggers were added for both push and pull_request paths. Exact code/workflow head: `dea77de9896bbf4be067029609099b4020c53e3b`. `get_commit_combined_status` currently returned no status contexts; the connected `fetch_commit_workflow_runs` action returns PR-triggered runs only and returned none for this push; public Actions page access was unavailable in this session. Record the new push workflow outcome as **PENDING / NOT VERIFIED**, not PASS. Previous switch_gsw source-contract run `42534963105` remains SUCCESS on exact SHA `e2d94abcec416d9394aa82a3ade72d4af5657451`.
- **Validation limits:** L1 source/ABI/call-path review PASS; local L2 focused host compile/parser regression PASS as qualified above; new exact-head Actions L2 result PENDING / NOT VERIFIED. No MIPS/uClibc cross-compile, full firmware/TRX, live Clause 45 ioctl operation or physical switch/router validation was run. Switch tools remain AUDITING; this bounded fix does not close Wireless or Firewall/NAT.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Close DHCPv6 DUID symlink and short-write hazards (2026-11-09 05:57 CST)

- **Repository / branch:** Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2, main. Required conversation title remains Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删.
- **Recovered task:** resumed the IPv6 Firewall/NAT parity/failure-handling rotation from the previous checkpoint. Mapped the core IPv6 filter/default-filter, mangle and NAT restore writers in trunk/user/rc/src/firewall_ex.c; their snapshots/rollback are handled by the shared family-aware firewall restore transaction. The early boot gate in rc.c requires the IPv6 default filter whenever IPv6 is enabled. full_restart_ipv6() disables forwarding and rebuilds firewall on disable; on enable it calls full_restart_wan(), so the suspected generic IPv6-enable firewall-refresh gap was not demonstrated. The shipped miniupnpd package currently sets ENABLE_IPV6:=0; do not treat its pinhole code as an active IPv6 ruleset writer without revisiting that build configuration. Firewall/NAT remains AUDITING; this was a bounded map, not subsystem closure.
- **P1 security finding in DHCPv6 identity persistence:** start_dhcp6c() wrote the predictable /tmp/dhcp6c_duid by unlinking and then calling fopen(..., "w"). Since /tmp is shared-writable, a local process able to race this path could replace it with a symlink between unlink and open and cause the privileged RC process to truncate/write a different file. The BusyBox DHCPv6 fallback reader/writer had the same unsafe pathname trust: ordinary open(O_RDONLY) followed symlinks, its O_CREAT|O_TRUNC call omitted the required mode argument, and it accepted short writev() results as success. It also treated descriptor 0 as unopened through fd > 0.
- **Implementation:** commit 077c17adb49bce9c8a1636e31a01228fbe107b8d adds a shared DUID safe-open policy and regression, then uses it in both RC and BusyBox paths. Commit 7dd3986ef682e3e58ba58fed981dbac9cdc1d371 changes the header helpers to static inline after a focused -Werror compile found the unused-helper warning that would occur in the RC translation unit.
- **Fix details:** existing DUIDs are opened with O_NOFOLLOW|O_NONBLOCK, then checked by fstat() for regular-file type, current effective-UID ownership, single link, no group/world write permission, and bounded size. New files use O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW with mode 0600, so a path raced into existence is rejected rather than followed/truncated. RC checks all three writes and fclose(); BusyBox validates the stored length against the exact file size, uses fd >= 0, checks the exact writev() byte count and removes an incomplete file it created. A BusyBox in-memory DUID remains usable for that run if exclusive persistence loses a race.
- **Changed paths:** trunk/user/shared/src/dhcp6c_duid_file_policy.h; trunk/user/shared/src/tests/dhcp6c_duid_file_policy.c; trunk/user/rc/src/net_wan6.c; trunk/user/busybox/busybox-1.24.x/networking/udhcp/dhcp6c_common.c; .github/workflows/httpd-security.yml (symmetric push/PR path filters and the new source-contract/regression step).
- **Host validation:** GNU89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror DUID policy regression PASS; ASan/UBSan with leak detection PASS. An additional single-caller include check confirmed static inline avoids the unused-static-function warning under -Werror.
- **Exact-head GitHub Actions:** run [42527600055](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42527600055), exact head 7dd3986ef682e3e58ba58fed981dbac9cdc1d371, completed SUCCESS. New step "Check DHCPv6 DUID file safety" passed; all other workflow steps also completed successfully. The earlier code-only run [42527564293](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42527564293) on 077c17adb49bce9c8a1636e31a01228fbe107b8d also completed SUCCESS, but the final verified code head is the later 7dd3986e...
- **Validation boundary:** L1 source/dataflow review and L2 focused host regression + exact-head Actions PASS. The workflow does not build the complete production RC or BusyBox translation units. MIPS/uClibc, complete firmware/TRX, live DHCPv6 lease/IA_PD interoperability, race testing on router /tmp, and physical hardware validation remain NOT RUN. Do not mark IPv6 Firewall/NAT or DHCPv6 subsystem fully audited from this one fix.
- **Exact next task:** resume IPv6 Firewall/NAT parity review, not the DUID patch. Complete the remaining ruleset writer/caller inventory and compare IPv6 NAT/mangle/filter semantics, module-availability gates and all restore-failure/forwarding outcomes against IPv4. In particular, trace who enables and owns ip6table_nat, the unconditional ULA FULLCONENAT rule, and extension hooks after core table restore; only implement a further fix when a concrete path is evidenced. Keep Firewall/NAT and Wireless AUDITING. The separate trunk/user/utils/src/switch/ CLI/register audit also remains open.
- **Do not redo:** DUID safe-open/creation, exact-length/short-write checks, its workflow contract, the storage NFS export publication, staged per-device/global unmount inventories, or their verified tests. Do not claim a full IPv6 firewall audit, full firmware build or hardware testing.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Complete global storage inventories before fallback swapoff/unmount (2026-11-09 05:38 CST)

- **Repository / branch:** Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2, main. Conversation title remains Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删.
- **Recovered task completed:** after the per-device umount_dev() hardening (implementation 806cd75c89f047b865d9b11677f8a819f12cb936, exact-head corrected CI 42525420578 SUCCESS 40/40), followed the previous handoff into libdisk's global fallback umount_all_storage().
- **P2 finding:** global cleanup previously called swapoff() inside the /proc/swaps read loop, checking read/close failures only afterward. It then parsed /proc/mounts with an sscanf that silently skipped malformed/truncated rows and ran umount() while the mount file was still being read. A late read/fclose error or malformed row could therefore be discovered after earlier swap or mount side effects. It also continued unmounting mounts after a swapoff failure, risking release of an active swap backing filesystem.
- **Implementation commit:** 27b94e9b7ad7e7d73a2a2c2db94aaa3aa79868f0 — stages both complete inventories in linked lists; validates newline-terminated, bounded, exact five-field /proc/swaps header/data rows and six-field /proc/mounts rows; checks read, fclose and allocation errors; performs no side effect unless both inventories are complete. It then attempts all swapoffs first; if any swapoff fails, it frees both lists and skips every unmount. Only successful swapoff allows the staged storage mount loop. Lists are freed on all success/error paths.
- **Changed paths:** trunk/user/libdisk/src/disk_initial.c; trunk/user/libdisk/src/storage_swap_policy.h; trunk/user/rc/src/tests/storage_swap_policy.c; .github/workflows/httpd-security.yml.
- **Workflow-contract correction:** initial Actions run [42525975164](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42525975164) on the code commit failed in the existing swap-inventory source contract because it still expected storage_swap_header_valid()/storage_swap_first_field() calls directly inside umount_all_storage(). The new policy executable printed PASS; the stale source assertion then failed. Workflow-only correction commit 7ed4a755c7d71b95ff92e7bb487af6a42861fbd1 updates the assertions to the new staging parsers, read/close checks, swapoff gate and side-effect order.
- **Exact-head Actions:** run [42526075706](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42526075706), exact head 7ed4a755c7d71b95ff92e7bb487af6a42861fbd1, completed **SUCCESS (40/40 steps)**. All storage steps passed, including safe-eject/spindown, mount inventory, ejected-device inventory, and swap/global-cleanup inventory.
- **Host validation:** GNU89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror policy regression PASS; GNU89 ASan/UBSan with leak detection PASS. Pre-update source order contract PASS. This was a focused policy test, not a production libdisk translation-unit build.
- **Safe-remove caller mapping:** rc.c exposes ejata -> safe_remove_stor_device(ATA...), ejmmc -> safe_remove_stor_device(MMC...), and ejall -> safe_remove_all_stor_devices(). The inspected hotplug_stor.c and hotplug_usb.c files had no direct safe_remove_stor_device() call. Do not infer a whole-tree caller closure from this limited file check. Keep storage **AUDITING**.
- **Validation boundary:** complete production disk_initial.c/RC translation-unit build, MIPS/uClibc, full firmware/TRX, actual swapoff/unmount runtime, NFS service integration, and physical router/storage testing remain **NOT RUN**.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Stage complete mount inventory before per-device unmount (2026-11-09 05:27 CST)

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Guard WAN VLAN setup against failed switch PVID reads (2026-11-09 04:21 CST)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Recovered task / base:** resumed the exact next action from the previous HTTPD switch-status checkpoint. Base main HEAD was `5196541495674cce0173c3c3e8882d53966adb0b`; implementation, regression and workflow commit is `f73b64108617772a8614fbbb7aaa89a69d2768cf`.
- **Bounded caller inventory:** enumerated and scanned all 36 C translation units in `trunk/user/rc/src/`. Within that directory, direct `phy_vlan_pvid_wan_get()` consumers were in `net_wan.c` and `net_lan.c`; `get_vlan_vid_wan()` was consumed in two Hardware NAT paths in `net.c`. The scan is limited to the RC C directory; it does not prove repository-wide or external API closure.
- **Finding (P2 availability / invalid configuration):** the MTK/RTL PVID getter now correctly returns negative errno on failed ioctl, but its callers still treated the result as a VLAN ID. WAN CPU/IPTV setup could use the negative error in VLAN TX mapping, interface-name generation or VLAN creation; Hardware NAT could format a negative `wan_vid` module argument; and a failed before/after PVID comparison could spuriously force a WAN full restart.
- **Fix:** `get_vlan_vid_wan()` now propagates negative errors and rejects invalid positive PVIDs. `config_vinet_wan()` and `launch_viptv_wan()` only query the switch when a fallback is actually needed, validate the PVID before using it, and return an error before mapping/creating VLAN interfaces. WAN startup aborts its unsafe primary VLAN setup and removes callback links on failure; IPTV setup failure is logged and isolated so primary WAN startup can continue. Hardware NAT samples the WAN VID once and skips load/update on lookup error, retaining any existing loaded parameter. LAN switch reconfiguration restarts WAN only when both PVID samples are successful and differ; failed samples are logged and never interpreted as a VLAN change.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/rc/src/{net_wan.c,net.c,net_lan.c,switch_status_policy.h,tests/switch_status_policy.c}`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml` (push/PR filters and source contracts).
- **Regression / exact-head CI:** extended the GNU89 switch-status policy test for explicit-VID compatibility, successful fallback, negative/invalid PVID rejection without output mutation, and safe before/after comparison. Actions run [42519616962](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42519616962), exact head `f73b64108617772a8614fbbb7aaa89a69d2768cf`, completed **SUCCESS (39/39)**. It compiled and ran the actual policy regression with `-std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror`, then passed the RC WAN/LAN/Hardware-NAT source contracts and symmetric push/PR filter checks.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 bounded RC caller/source review and L2 host policy regression + exact-head Actions PASS. The workflow does not compile complete production `net_wan.c`, `net.c` or `net_lan.c` objects. MIPS/uClibc, full firmware/TRX, WAN integration and physical switch/router tests are **NOT RUN**. Wireless and switch remain **AUDITING**.
- **Residual inventory:** `trunk/user/utils/src/switch/` remains an independent CLI/register-tool surface not covered by the RC PVID caller fix. The broader Wireless audit also retains association/driver ioctl consumers, supplicant lifecycle, credentials/config boundaries and external consumers outside the bounded source scan.
- **Exact first action next conversation:** re-fetch main and rotate to the IPv6 Firewall/NAT policy audit. Map IPv6 filter/NAT initialization, every ruleset writer and restore/rollback path (start with `firewall_ex.c`, `rc.c` and the existing firewall policy helpers); compare its failure gates and safe defaults with the already-tested IPv4 startup/transaction path. ## Latest resumable checkpoint — Normalize HTTPD MTK/RTL switch ioctl failures and counter publication (2026-11-09 04:01 CST)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Main before this governance-record update:** `d5b0b76372bcec25f698ed9a0dc8408ee8f2044e`. This exact source/workflow head has matching successful Actions run 42517880293; re-fetch `main` before the next write.
- **Recovered scope / expanded inventory:** continued the previous whole-user-tree switch status/counter caller audit beyond the 36 C translation units in `trunk/user/rc/src/`. The repository also has a separate HTTPD switch backend in `trunk/user/httpd/src/switch_mtk_esw.c` and `switch_rtl8367.c`, consumed through `web_ex.c`; these are not the RC switch implementations already covered by `switch_status_policy.h`.
- **Finding (P1/P2 status reliability and disclosure):** both HTTPD switch ioctl wrappers returned positive `errno` on open/ioctl failure and ignored `close()` errors. Their port-byte getters only rejected negative errors, left `port_bytes_t pb` uninitialized and then copied its RX/TX fields to the caller. On failed ioctl, the positive error could therefore be mistaken for success and uninitialized stack bytes could be emitted as Ethernet counters by `web_ex.c`'s RStats endpoint. The speed-status getter had the same positive-error mismatch and could report "No Link" rather than "I/O Error".
- **Fix:** introduced `httpd_switch_ioctl_policy.h`; MTK and RTL wrappers now snapshot `errno` immediately, normalize open/ioctl/close failures to negative errno (using `-EIO` if errno is absent), preserve the ioctl error over a later close error, and report close failure only after a successful ioctl. Both port-byte getters reject NULL outputs, zero the local ioctl structure, and publish RX/TX only after a successful result; the speed-status callers treat every nonzero result as an I/O failure.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/httpd/src/{switch_mtk_esw.c,switch_rtl8367.c,httpd_switch_ioctl_policy.h}`, `trunk/user/httpd/src/tests/httpd_switch_ioctl_policy.c`, `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml` (symmetric push/PR filters and a new host regression/source-contract step).
- **Implementation and correction commits:** `b77b6779e55cc41c325f012df7bd54fe3d7b975b` introduced the bounded fix/test/CI step; post-commit source review found duplicated function-signature text in two declarations, corrected in `2ace8fabbf9cbe2ace9580143db7e0b87e66eed3`, which also added exact-one-definition source guards. Workflow runs 42517743357 and 42517811258 both reached the new step, the GNU89 helper executable printed PASS, but the Python source contract failed because its expected ternary expression omitted the parentheses present in the helper. `d5b0b76372bcec25f698ed9a0dc8408ee8f2044e` corrects that assertion.
- **Exact-head Actions:** run [42517880293](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42517880293), exact head `d5b0b76372bcec25f698ed9a0dc8408ee8f2044e`, completed **SUCCESS 39/39**. The new step compiles/runs the GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` policy test and verifies MTK/RTL errno snapshots, operation-vs-close precedence, pointer checks, zero-initialization, output-publication wiring, speed error handling, the `web_ex.c` negative-error caller, and both workflow filters. Local GNU89 and ASan/UBSan runs of the policy regression also printed PASS.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 source and caller review PASS; L2 host policy test + ASan/UBSan and exact-head Actions PASS. The workflow does not compile either full production HTTPD switch translation unit; MIPS/uClibc, full firmware/TRX, and live MTK/RTL hardware tests are **NOT RUN**. This closes only the duplicate HTTPD status/counter failure path, not the broader Wireless/HTTPD audits.
- **Residual inventory:** `trunk/user/utils/src/switch/` contains independent register/CLI tools and is not covered by this HTTPD fix. The earlier RC-side change made `phy_vlan_pvid_wan_get()` return negative errno; `net_wan.c` has known consumers in `get_vlan_vid_wan()` and WAN VLAN setup around the prior inspected lines 487 and 620. Their treatment of a negative PVID is the next concrete risk to review.
- **Remote persistence:** all implementation, follow-up corrections, tests and workflow changes are on `main`; the exact tested main head is `d5b0b76372bcec25f698ed9a0dc8408ee8f2044e`. The earlier source-contract failures are recorded above and were corrected; do not treat the failed heads as validated.
- **Exact first action next conversation:** re-fetch `main`; inspect every `phy_vlan_pvid_wan_get()` caller in `trunk/user/rc/src/net_wan.c` (including `get_vlan_vid_wan()`, CPU VLAN setup and IPTV VLAN setup). ## Latest resumable checkpoint — Harden wireless-tools scan event and ESSID lengths (2026-11-09 03:15 CST)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Main before this governance-record commit:** `5cd0396df804f7bc0eefd632c3379d85ed9a4c7a` (contains the implementation commit and the CI-contract correction). Re-fetch live `main` before any next write.
- **Recovered task / source ownership:** the previous handoff named `net_wifi.c` / `ralink.c` as likely scan-parser owners. Source inspection found that the Wireless Extensions scan-event parser actually lives in `trunk/user/wireless-tools/src/iwlib.c::iw_extract_event_stream()`, with CLI scan request construction in `iwlist.c::print_scanning_info()`. `net_wifi.c` and `ralink.c` do not own this event parser; continue their separate ioctl/association audit rather than treating the wireless subsystem as closed.
- **Finding (P2 memory-safety / scan reliability):** the event decoder trusted the kernel/driver-provided `iwe->len` without first bounding it to the remaining ioctl buffer or the known event's fixed header size. It also measured fixed payload reads against the entire remaining stream rather than the current event, so a short event could consume bytes from the next event. A point event could advertise a payload beyond the returned buffer and hand an invalid pointer/length to consumers such as `iwlist`. Separately, `iwlist` copied arbitrary command-line ESSID length into the fixed 32-byte `iw_scan_req.essid` field without a limit.
- **Implementation:** added `iw_scan_bounds.h` helpers; the decoder validates stream pointers, frame length against available bytes, known fixed-header length, current-event end and compatibility-adjusted fixed reads; malformed event pointers are cleared and parsing stops at the event boundary. Point payload length is now derived from the checked event end. `iwlist` rejects an ESSID larger than `sizeof(scanopt.essid)` before assigning its 8-bit length or copying bytes.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/wireless-tools/src/{iwlib.c,iwlist.c,iw_scan_bounds.h}`, `trunk/user/wireless-tools/src/tests/iw_scan_bounds.c`, `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Implementation / correction commits:** `205b32b1f040bd3e930f9fb28a715527d4b59949` contains the code, regression, and push/PR workflow integration. `5cd0396df804f7bc0eefd632c3379d85ed9a4c7a` corrects the workflow assertion to inspect the fixed-payload copy rather than the intentionally earlier LCP-header copy, and removes distinct-pointer-type warnings in the test.
- **CI failure/correction:** first exact-head run [42513929518](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42513929518) on `205b32b1f040bd3e930f9fb28a715527d4b59949` ran the sanitizer regression successfully (it printed `Wireless scan event and ESSID bounds: PASS`) and compiled `iwlist.c`, but its new Python source contract failed because its ordering assertion matched the LCP-header copy. This was a test-harness assertion issue, not a C regression failure. The follow-up corrected that assertion and cleaned the test warnings.
- **Exact-head GitHub Actions:** run [42514055103](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42514055103) on exact head `5cd0396df804f7bc0eefd632c3379d85ed9a4c7a` completed **SUCCESS**, all **38/38** job steps passed. The new step compiled and ran `iwlib.c` plus the regression with GNU89, ASan/UBSan and leak detection; compiled the `iwlist.c` object; and checked both push and pull-request path filters plus source guard ordering.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 parser/callsite source review PASS; L2 Actions host sanitizer regression, `iwlist.c` object compilation and source contracts PASS. This is not a MIPS/uClibc target compile or a full firmware build. Full firmware/TRX, real radio/driver scan and physical-router validation are **NOT RUN**.
- **Remote persistence:** implementation and test are on `main`; only `main` was present in the branch inventory and no separate remote checkpoint branch was found. This checkpoint is documentation-only after the tested code/workflow head.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Retry switch link-state refresh after ioctl failures (2026-11-09 03:01 CST)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Main before this governance-record commit:** `fe2b21a85299a7101e7a8e2e15a2df5eb900dbed` (source/test/workflow commit). This record update follows it; re-fetch live `main` before the next write.
- **Recovered task / module:** resumed the previous wireless handoff's remaining switch link-status reliability item. Wireless remains **AUDITING**; this bounded caller fix does not close the switch or wireless module.
- **Call-site inventory:** scanned all 36 C translation units in `trunk/user/rc/src/` for `phy_status_port_link`, `phy_status_port_speed`, and `phy_status_port_bytes`. Calls found: `detect_link.c` polls link-change and LAN aggregate status; `net_wan.c` reads the configured WAN PHY; `rstats.c` reads port counters. No consumer of `phy_status_port_speed()` was found in that directory scan. This is an RC source-directory inventory, not a claim that every user-space directory or external consumer was exhaustively scanned.
- **Finding (P1/P2 availability / stale state):** `detect_link.c::dl_on_timer()` ignored the return from `phy_status_port_link_changed()`. Since `is_link_changed` was initialized to zero, an ioctl failure after initialization suppressed the WAN/LAN refresh. In addition, failures from the subsequent WAN or LAN status reads left the prior values cached without scheduling another attempt. The detector could therefore retain stale link state until an unrelated future notification.
- **Implementation:** added host-testable `switch_status_link_refresh_required()` and `switch_status_link_refresh_failed()` policies. `dl_on_timer()` now refreshes on first initialization, a reported change, a failed change-notification ioctl, or a previously pending retry. It retains the retry marker while the change query, WAN read, or LAN aggregate read fails, and clears it only when all three succeed. Existing state is still published only after each corresponding read succeeds.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/rc/src/detect_link.c`, `trunk/user/rc/src/switch_status_policy.h`, `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/switch_status_policy.c`, `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Local validation:** GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` focused host regression PASS; ASan/UBSan with leak detection PASS. Coverage includes initial refresh, unchanged fast path, pending retry, changed event, change-query failure, WAN-read failure, LAN-read failure and successful WAN up/down samples.
- **Exact-head GitHub Actions:** run [42512806703](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42512806703), exact head `fe2b21a85299a7101e7a8e2e15a2df5eb900dbed`, completed **SUCCESS**; its single job completed all **37/37** steps successfully, including the switch policy compile/run and source contract. The source-contract step checks retry wiring in production `detect_link.c`.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 control-flow review PASS; L2 focused GNU89 and ASan/UBSan host regression PASS; exact-head Actions PASS (37/37). The Actions source contract does not compile the complete production `detect_link.c` translation unit. - **Remote persistence:** all four implementation/test/workflow paths are committed to `main` as `fe2b21a85299a7101e7a8e2e15a2df5eb900dbed`; no local-only patch and no separate checkpoint branch was created. This governance-record commit follows that source commit.

Only main exists; no checkpoint branch is required.
- **Source + regression commit:** b9845d577186940f8d0384cccceeb6910fbb34e9 — fix(wireless): normalize ioctl and socket cleanup errors. It changes ralink.c, adds wireless_ioctl_policy.h and tests/wireless_ioctl_policy.c, and adds the policy test/source contract to the Actions workflow.

- **Finding / code change:** the file-static wl_ioctl() has one direct local caller, get_apcli_connected(), which treats every non-negative return as a successful query. Workflow compilation uses cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror; existing command-status and restore-transaction tests continue to run. Static assertions verify production status propagation, gate-before-WAN ordering and both family forwarding guards.

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.

- **Recovered scope:** the previous Adbyby checkpoint explicitly directed the next session to continue the Firewall/NAT audit, with emphasis on IPv4/IPv6 restore result propagation, rollback boundaries and avoiding activation of an unapplied policy. The Adbyby set lifecycle and VPN lease lifecycle were already completed and were not reopened.
- **Finding (P1 stability / security):** `start_firewall_ex()` already aborted a failed built-in restore transaction and rolled the touched tables back. When rollback itself succeeded, however, the code merely skipped extension hooks and then fell through to `set_ipv4_forward(1)`. A successful rollback restores the previous firewall rules; it does not mean that the new requested ruleset was applied. The fall-through could enable IPv4 forwarding on a rebuild whose new policy never committed (particularly unsafe during initial setup when there may be no previously activated policy).
- **Fix:** the built-in-restore-failure branch now returns immediately after logging. This preserves the forwarding state that existed alongside the restored previous rules and prevents IPv4 forwarding activation, PPPoE multi-WAN detection and module cleanup from proceeding against an unapplied policy. The existing incomplete-rollback path continues to disable IPv4 and IPv6 forwarding and retain recovery snapshots.

- **Regression coverage:** the existing `tests/firewall_restore_transaction.c` host regression already exercises an apply failure followed by successful reverse rollback and asserts that the transaction remains failed while `rollback_failed` is false. The workflow source contract now additionally requires a return between the built-in-restore-failure branch and `set_ipv4_forward(1)`, and checks those transaction-state assertions.
- **Actions status / honesty boundary:** the source and workflow changes are committed, but the exact-head push Actions result for `0b886c2ede22c9561a8c9ed0caa69ce01f4a5df5` is **NOT VERIFIED** at this checkpoint. Do not describe this new fix as CI-PASS until the exact SHA is independently confirmed in Actions. The preceding Adbyby tested source head `32e45d81e0e1109f879850eeabc59a2a4ebe1686` remains verified by run [42502289762](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42502289762), but that run predates this fix.

- **Exact-head GitHub Actions:** [run 42502289762](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42502289762) completed **SUCCESS** on `head_sha=32e45d81e0e1109f879850eeabc59a2a4ebe1686`; all **35/35** steps passed. The job executed the Adbyby snapshot/setup rollback regression, Adbyby transactional restore policy regression, and firewall/Adbyby source contracts.
- **Recovered task / finding:** the previous checkpoint explicitly queued Adbyby `blockip` ipset cold-boot and firewall-rebuild ordering. `ipset -! restore` was fed a `create blockip` line followed by entries. On a rebuild, the existing set makes `create` idempotently succeed without clearing old elements; removed rows therefore remained in the live set and could continue dropping destinations that were no longer in the persistent block list.
- **Production fix:** `adbyby_refresh_blockip_set()` now builds a uniquely named temporary `hash:net family inet` set from the full configured list using a mode-0600 private staging file. A failed file generation, set creation or restore discards only the stage and leaves the previous stable set untouched. When `blockip` exists, `ipset swap stage blockip` replaces its contents while preserving the stable name referenced by iptables, then the old set under the staging name is destroyed. On cold boot, the fully populated set is published with `ipset rename stage blockip`. The narrow script action `B` refreshes only this set and skips unrelated NVRAM initialization writes. The firewall Adbyby preflight now runs that refresh action and confirms `blockip` exists before restoring the saved Adbyby rules.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/adbyby/adbyby.sh`; `trunk/user/adbyby/tests/firewall_snapshot.sh`; `trunk/user/rc/src/firewall_ex.c`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Regression coverage / result:** the stateful fake-ipset host harness verifies replacement removes an old entry, a partial restore failure leaves the previous live set unchanged, cold boot publishes by rename, and successful paths leave no staging-set leak. Existing Adbyby setup rollback, private snapshot and transactional restore checks remain enabled. Actions passed shell syntax checks, the host shell regressions and the GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` Adbyby restore-policy test/source-contract steps.
- **CI correction history:** intermediate exact-head runs exposed real defects in the evolving regression harness: the fake restore stub initially returned a false failure on success; the fake `list` branch later fell through a final success exit and falsely reported a missing set as present, making the cold-boot test take the swap path. Both are corrected. The initial stage identifier was also revised to derive from the unique temporary filename rather than shell PID interpolation. Do not treat those red runs as proof of a production-kernel failure; the exact final run above is green.
- **Validation boundary / residuals:** the fake ipset harness is a host model, not execution against the router kernel ipset implementation. The workflow checks production source wiring and compiles/runs the standalone restore policy regression; it does **not** build the complete production `firewall_ex.c` RC translation unit. MIPS/uClibc target build, full firmware/TRX, live kernel `ipset swap/rename` with active iptables references, real firewall rebuild/cold-boot, router runtime and hardware tests are **NOT RUN**. Firewall/NAT remains **AUDITING**; this closes one Adbyby set lifecycle defect, not the subsystem.
- **Remote persistence:** final exact-tested source/test/workflow HEAD is `32e45d81e0e1109f879850eeabc59a2a4ebe1686`. Only remote branch `main` exists; no separate checkpoint branch is used. This governance update is committed on top of the tested head; there is no local-only patch.

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.

- **Production + regression commit:** `3922f16daf7d4147e08f871369f589658b56c77d` — `fix(vpn): serialize lease inventory lifecycle`.
- **Workflow-only follow-ups:** `a46bded7e17118cdde2261f99179136cf83fe1e6` corrected the restore-lock ordering assertion; `b8cd52d406d42213eb182832b546d98543e9a891` aligned the PPP interface assertion with the validated interface array; `f66c6d7288922fb7955396201b48a705150074b8` updated legacy lease source contracts; `665a23cab0a340393270cac445acfab16f7f9dd1` repaired the workflow indentation and is the final exact-tested source/test/workflow HEAD.
- **Exact-head Actions:** [run 42500281239](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42500281239) completed **SUCCESS** on `head_sha=665a23cab0a340393270cac445acfab16f7f9dd1`. All **35/35** job steps passed, including the VPN lease lifecycle source contract, firewall token/restore checks, Adbyby firewall setup checks, switch telemetry, DNSMasq and HTTPD auth/route checks.
- **Recovered task:** previous handoff explicitly required a stable root-private advisory lock shared by ip-up, ip-down, start/stop reset and the firewall VPN_LIST reader; checked firewall-add and append/flush/close handling with safe rollback; and complete-inventory validation before applying VPN_LIST rules. The previous task's fail-closed disconnect-prune change was already complete and retained.
- **Implementation:** added `vpn_server_lease_lock.h`, using a persistent private `/tmp/.vpns.leases.lock` inode, `O_NOFOLLOW`/`O_CLOEXEC`, owner/mode/link checks and blocking `fcntl(F_SETLKW)`. The lock inode is never unlinked. ip-up validates its event tokens and peer record, takes the lock before adding the direct firewall permission, appends through a checked `O_APPEND` descriptor, validates the existing inventory, writes the complete record and `fsync()`s it. Append, flush-equivalent write, sync and close failures propagate; rollback verifies the original device/inode and expected post-append size before truncating. If append fails after the firewall rule was added, the direct permission is removed best-effort and the callback reports failure. ip-down holds the same lock across direct rule deletion and fail-closed pruning. Start/stop lease-file clearing also uses this lock.
- **Firewall consumer consistency:** `firewall_ex.c::include_vpns_clients()` no longer uses permissive `fscanf`. It validates the complete root-owned private inventory, bounds every record, checks each PPP interface and stream close before emitting any VPN_LIST rules. The consumer transfers lock ownership to `ipt_filter_rules()`; the lock stays held through staged `iptables-restore` application and is released after the restore file is removed. This closes the stale-snapshot race where a new ip-up direct rule could otherwise be overwritten by a concurrently rebuilt old VPN_LIST snapshot. Incomplete inventory aborts the staged core restore.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/rc/src/vpn_server.c`, `trunk/user/rc/src/firewall_ex.c`, new `trunk/user/rc/src/vpn_server_lease_lock.h`, `trunk/user/rc/src/vpn_server_lease_policy.h`, `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/vpn_server_lease_policy.c`, `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.

The workflow compiled and executed the committed VPN policy regression and validated source ordering/path contracts on the exact tested HEAD. - **CI correction history:** intermediate runs exposed stale workflow assertions left over from the old append helper and old single-interface reader, then a lock-order assertion and YAML indentation issue. Those were workflow-contract errors, not a reported regression failure in the VPN policy executable. The final exact-head run above passed all 35 steps.
- **Validation boundary / residuals:** Actions compiles and runs the standalone policy/lock regression and checks production source wiring; it does **not** compile the full production `vpn_server.c` or `firewall_ex.c` translation units. MIPS/uClibc target build, full firmware/TRX, router VPN reconnect/disconnect stress, actual iptables runtime and hardware validation are **NOT RUN**. - **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Base main HEAD for this task:** `bb304d66ff7547bab119a8f2c867830ebd629465`.
- **Production implementation:** `4541cfb7f4eb788981544a4e48ddbea8572f6b90`.
- **Workflow correction commits:** `b1aa598bdaabb43addcaa6275baa20e651347a99` (shell continuation and escaped source boundaries); final source-contract correction `8245d15e04822f76e99ae74fa54a300d1276af28`.

Logs confirm both the new restart-policy test and existing DNSMasq file trust-boundary test PASS.
- **Recovered task:** prior DNS handoff directed us to audit `restart_dhcpd()`, `stop_dns_dhcpd()` and `start_dns_dhcpd()`. The old restart stopped the daemon before generating replacement configuration, so any preparation error could leave the old daemon stopped even though its previous main config was preserved.
- **Implementation:** `prepare_dns_dhcpd()` builds files and validates upstream DNS snapshot without stopping/launching. Public `start_dns_dhcpd()` prepares, then launches once only when service is enabled. `restart_dhcpd()` records whether dnsmasq was running, prepares first, then uses `dnsmasq_restart_policy_action()`: failed preparation keeps an existing daemon untouched, or aborts when there was no daemon; a successful disabled configuration stops without relaunch; a successful enabled configuration stops, rechecks process inventory, and launches once. If dnsmasq remains after stop, return `EBUSY` and do not duplicate it. Unsafe/missing DNS server snapshot causes staged main config cleanup before publication; a safe previous server snapshot can be retained after refresh failure.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/rc/src/services_ex.c`; new `trunk/user/rc/src/dnsmasq_restart_policy.h`; new `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/dnsmasq_restart_policy.c`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`; this round updates `dev_log.md`, `REFACTOR_ROADMAP.md`, and `SESSION_HANDOFF.md`.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Normalize MTK/RTL switch port-counter I/O failures (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`. Re-fetch live `main` before every subsequent write.
- **Base main HEAD:** `a1bb5a8d41b6c4c237e9af42ad612b403b27fdd3`. **Implementation/test/workflow commit:** `646c29081e07359391e830b89929f14a5abacdb8`. **Pull-request path-filter correction:** `d943c4a138afce543bef45c4e362dccedf2871aa`.
- **Recovered exact task:** completed the previous handoff's requested audit of `switch_mtk_esw.c` and `switch_rtl8367.c`: return conventions for open/ioctl, `phy_status_port_bytes()` publication, the `rstats.c` caller and descriptor close handling.
- **Confirmed P1/P2 telemetry reliability defect:** `mtk_esw_ioctl()` / `rtl8367_ioctl()` returned positive `errno` on open/ioctl failure, but each `phy_status_port_bytes()` checked only `ioctl_result < 0`. It then copied a potentially uninitialized local `port_bytes_t` into `rx`/`tx` and returned the positive errno. The actual `rstats.c` caller also checks only `< 0`, so the error was treated as valid port traffic data rather than skipped. Both ioctl wrappers also ignored `close(fd)` failure.
- **Implementation:** added `switch_status_policy.h`. `switch_status_publish_port_bytes()` rejects null output pointers, normalizes positive errno to negative errno while preserving already-negative errors, and writes counters only on zero status. Both switch drivers now validate the output pointers, zero-initialize their local `port_bytes_t` before ioctl, and use the shared publication policy. Both ioctl wrappers now report close failure when no earlier open/ioctl error already exists; an earlier primary error is preserved.
- **Changed paths:** updated `trunk/user/rc/src/switch_mtk_esw.c` and `switch_rtl8367.c`; added `trunk/user/rc/src/switch_status_policy.h` and `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/switch_status_policy.c`; updated `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml` push and pull-request path filters and regression/source-contract step. `rstats.c` and `switch.h` were inspected as caller/API contracts, not modified.
- **Host regression:** `cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror` compile/run PASS; AddressSanitizer/UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer with leak detection PASS. The regression covers successful RX/TX publication, positive errno normalization (`EIO`, `ENODEV`, `EBADF`), preservation of already-negative errors, and null output-pointer rejection without modifying the other output.
- **GitHub Actions:** initial exact implementation head `646c29081e07359391e830b89929f14a5abacdb8`, run [42491291207](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42491291207), compiled and passed the new C regression and preceding checks, but failed its new source-contract step because six paths were missing from `pull_request.paths`. Corrected in workflow-only commit `d943c4a138afce543bef45c4e362dccedf2871aa`. Exact corrected-head run [42491385478](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42491385478) completed **SUCCESS**; all workflow functional steps passed. Its log confirms `Switch port-byte error propagation policy: PASS` and `MTK/RTL switch ioctl error normalization, close handling, counter output and caller contracts: OK`.
- **Validation boundary / residuals:** scoped source review, GNU89 host regression and sanitizer pass. The Actions source-contract step checks both driver call paths, close-result handling, the `rstats.c` `<0` caller, and symmetric push/PR filters; host tests policy logic, not real switch ioctls. It does not compile the production switch-driver translation units. MIPS/uClibc, full firmware/TRX, actual switch hardware and live traffic counters NOT RUN. The switch and broader wireless rows remain **AUDITING**. Other link/speed/VLAN APIs still return their legacy positive errno convention and need independent caller-by-caller review; this round intentionally normalizes only the traffic-counter API that feeds the `<0` caller. A close failure is checked by source contract but not fault-injected against a real device descriptor.
- **Remote state:** implementation and workflow correction are on `main`; no local-only patch or checkpoint branch was created. The old wireless-config atomic publication checkpoint remains below as historical context. Re-fetch `main` before every new write.
- **Exact next task:** rotate to DNS / DHCP / resolver integration rather than stay in switch/HTTPD. Trace `dnsmasq_file.h` / its host regression, RC dnsmasq service startup/restart and generated resolver-file ownership/error propagation. Select one bounded P1/P2 stability defect around generated-file publication, restart rollback, or upstream resolver handling; add a focused GNU89 test and source contracts. Keep DNS marked PARTIAL/AUDITING, and keep Wireless, NVRAM, WAN/PPP, firewall and storage rows open. Do not claim firmware or hardware validation.

## Previous checkpoint — Atomic Ralink wireless configuration publication and restart failure gates (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`. Re-fetch live `main` before every subsequent write.
- **Base `main` HEAD:** `414eb1ea69bee85efa2f5faf136d9bfcff5ca3a6`. **Production implementation commit:** `c1cde95a6a066a7e701eb11b687ff53473924f32`. **Workflow assertion correction and final exact code/test/workflow head:** `a8c2ad427d4c63c7e32da91794a6b151a9e470cf`.
- **Recovered task:** previous checkpoint explicitly rotated the work to the inventoried-but-unaudited wireless management / MTK interface area. Reviewed `net_wifi.c`, `ralink.c`, `rc.c`, startup, radio restart and notification call paths. Also began a wider scan of MTK switch wrappers; a concrete follow-up candidate is recorded below, but it has not yet been fixed.
- **Confirmed P1/P2 stability defect:** `gen_ralink_config()` opened the live RT2860/iNIC wireless configuration with `fopen(..., "w+")`, truncating the previously working file before the replacement was fully generated. It did not check buffered write, flush or close errors, yet returned success after the attempted write. An overlay/disk-full or delayed I/O error could therefore leave a partial/empty driver configuration and destroy the last known-good file. Router startup and wireless restart paths also ignored generator failures and proceeded to load/restart radio services.
- **Implementation:** new `ralink_config_file.h` creates a same-directory `mkstemp` replacement, rejects non-regular existing targets, preserves an existing file's mode (new config defaults to owner-only 0600), checks `fflush`, `ferror`, `fsync` and `fclose`, then publishes only with same-filesystem `rename()`. Failed output/publish removes the temporary file and leaves the old configuration intact. `ralink.c` now uses this helper and reports publication failure. `restart_wifi_wl/rt()` return status and refuse to restart AP/WDS/AP-client services after configuration-generation failure. `init_router()` stops before wireless module loading if a required band config cannot be generated. PID 1 notification handling propagates radio/config generation failures and retains the in-flight completion marker rather than signaling false completion; CLI radio restart applets return the restart status.
- **Changed paths:** new `trunk/user/rc/src/ralink_config_file.h`, new `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/ralink_config_file.c`, updated `trunk/user/rc/src/ralink.c`, `net_wifi.c`, `rc.c`, `rc.h`, and `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Regression / validation:** host test built with `cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror` and passed; the same test also passed AddressSanitizer/UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer with leak detection. Cases cover successful atomic replacement and existing-mode preservation, 0600 mode for newly created configs, discard preserving the old file, rename failure cleanup, path-size rejection and delayed write failure via `/dev/full`. Workflow source contracts verify production wiring, startup/restart failure gates, notification completion semantics and symmetric push/PR path filters.
- **Exact-head GitHub Actions:** final exact head `a8c2ad427d4c63c7e32da91794a6b151a9e470cf`, run [42490501310](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42490501310), **SUCCESS**, all 33 listed steps successful. The new regression printed `Ralink wireless config atomic publication: PASS`; its source contract printed `Ralink atomic wireless config, startup/restart failure gates and notification completion contracts: OK`. Initial implementation head `c1cde95a6a066a7e701eb11b687ff53473924f32`, run [42490396133](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42490396133), was red only because the new workflow assertion sliced an if/else block too broadly; the standalone regression itself passed. The assertion was corrected and the exact final-head rerun above is green.
- **Validation boundary / residuals:** L1 scoped source review and L2 GNU89 host regression, sanitizer run and exact-head Actions PASS. Actions compiles/runs the standalone helper test and verifies source contracts; it does not compile production `rc.c`, `net_wifi.c` or `ralink.c`. No MIPS/uClibc production build, full firmware/TRX, router runtime or physical radio/hardware validation was run. The subsystem remains **AUDITING**, not closed: scan/association parsing, ioctl buffer/result contracts, wpa_supplicant/wireless-tools interactions, credential/config input boundaries, switch API lifecycles and restart/cleanup interactions remain to inspect. On a runtime generation failure the radio is deliberately left down and the incomplete notification marker is retained; no automatic retry loop was added, so a later explicit notification/restart is required. Parent-directory fsync after rename was not added, so this is atomic publication against partial-file replacement, not a claim of complete power-loss directory-entry durability.
- **Remote state:** final tested code/test/workflow head is committed on `main`; the final workflow-only correction is commit `a8c2ad427d4c63c7e32da91794a6b151a9e470cf`. No local-only patch or checkpoint branch exists. Always verify the live ref before writing.
- **Exact next task:** audit `trunk/user/rc/src/switch_mtk_esw.c` and `switch_rtl8367.c` ioctl result conventions. The current wrappers return positive `errno` on open/ioctl failure, but both `phy_status_port_bytes()` implementations test only `ioctl_result < 0` and then copy a local `port_bytes_t` buffer to caller outputs. Confirm all callers and contract expectations; if the mismatch is valid, normalize the error convention or test nonzero failure, initialize/avoid exposing output on failure, and add a GNU89 failure-injection regression plus workflow source contracts. Also audit close errors separately. Keep the broad wireless row **AUDITING**, and then continue scan/association parsing and other userland P1/P2 modules. NVRAM remains **AUDITING**; do not mark it closed.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Failure-safe all-column NVRAM group deletion (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Code/test/workflow HEAD before this governance refresh:** `e7a4316532e7acf6a95f60a92e799700b1cfb888`. Base main HEAD for this round: `83aacc03e4c34a6b8c87b76dbb1d161074fe3fa1`. The documentation refresh is committed immediately after the code/test/workflow head; always refetch live `main` before any subsequent write.
- **Recovered task:** previous handoff explicitly required audit of `nvram_del_list_map_x()` and `nvram_remove_group_item()` before selecting another module.
- **Confirmed P1 defect:** the old compaction helper ignored every `nvram_set()`, `nvram_set_temp()`, and `nvram_unset()` result. The caller ran columns sequentially and reduced the table-count key without checking any column result. A single NVRAM ioctl failure could leave different columns with different row mappings while advertising a smaller group count.
- **Implementation / code commit chain:** `41a69f9482a198d2f3554e813c88e7f962e23453` initial helper, production wiring, regression and workflow; `ceef851b60655f83151fb0f23a69239b6b58724b` declaration/trigger integration correction; `49b9b2c530af8cc09563d2ce63846bfe74283c1e` scoped workflow source-contract correction; `4f7a13db80bacf483a778be77f0da2a5f51fe0ea` snapshot/restore of count after a reported failed count write, including failure-after-apply regression; final `e7a4316532e7acf6a95f60a92e799700b1cfb888` frees count snapshot on success and adds the resource-release source contract.
- **Final changed paths:** `trunk/user/httpd/src/nvram_x.c`, `trunk/user/httpd/src/nvram_x.h`, `trunk/user/httpd/src/web_ex.c`, new `trunk/user/httpd/src/httpd_nvram_group_policy.h`, new `trunk/user/httpd/src/tests/httpd_nvram_group_policy.c`, and `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Behavior now:** `nvram_del_group_map_x()` validates the sorted map and column keys, snapshots all columns and cleanup rows plus each deleted-value cache and the original count, compacts every column with checked return values, cleans old rows only after all new column writes succeed, and publishes the reduced count last. If a write/unset/count operation reports failure it attempts to restore the original row values, caches and count. A separate result indicates if rollback was also incomplete. The CGI remove path propagates failure to an HTTP 500 and returns before normal value validation/redirect.
- **Regression coverage:** GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` host test cases cover single and multiple deletes, invalid maps, later-column set failure, deleted-cache failure, stale cleanup failure, count write failure before applying, and count write failure after applying. Workflow checks production wiring, write-result checks, count-after-columns ordering, rollback count restoration, snapshot release, CGI failure handling and push/PR path filters.
- **Exact-head Actions:** run [42485117467](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42485117467) completed **SUCCESS** on exact `head_sha=e7a4316532e7acf6a95f60a92e799700b1cfb888`. The new test printed `NVRAM group compaction policy: PASS`; its source contract printed `NVRAM all-column compaction, rollback gate, and CI path contracts: OK`. All workflow job steps passed. Prior exact code heads `49b9b2c5...` / run [42484962260](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42484962260) and `4f7a13db...` / run [42485063196](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42485063196) also passed. Two earlier integration heads had red CI and were corrected in subsequent commits; do not treat their failures as final state. Previous restore-durability head `e1b18baf9962562bb363258586df5e710c54933f` is also exact-head PASS at run [42474727990](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42474727990).
- **Validation boundary:** L1 source review PASS; L2 focused GNU89 host regression and exact-head Actions PASS. Full HTTPD production compile, MIPS/uClibc target build, complete firmware/TRX and L4 router/hardware validation **NOT RUN**.
- **Residual risks:** NVRAM/configuration persistence remains **AUDITING**. Rollback is best-effort rather than a kernel transaction; if rollback operations fail, data can remain partially compacted, but the code reports the incomplete rollback and tries to restore the count key. `nvram_get()` returns NULL for both absent keys and open/ioctl read failures, so the current API cannot distinguish them during snapshots. Prior restore-per-key SET rollback, non-atomic profile save, dirty-flag commit propagation, RC defaults/erase/startup commits and temporary-vs-persistent semantics remain open.
- **Remote state:** no local-only changes. Branch inventory at the code checkpoint showed only `main`; no checkpoint branch was needed because the atomic task and tests were committed. Final Actions state is SUCCESS for exact code/test/workflow SHA above.
- **Exact next task:** inspect definitions/callers of `asus_nvram_commit()` and `nvram_commit_safe()`; verify why the dirty flag is cleared before commit and why the commit return is ignored. Trace RC default restore, erase and startup commit callers. Pick one bounded persistence failure, add failure-injection/GNU89 regression and workflow source-contract coverage, then update all three project records. Keep NVRAM **AUDITING**; after this immediate persistence follow-up, rotate to other integrated userland P1/P2 modules and continue the parallel security queue. Full MIPS firmware build and physical-device validation remain later-stage milestones.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Require durable commit before NVRAM restore reboot (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`. Fetch the live `main` HEAD before every subsequent write.
- **Base code HEAD:** `69fcd2859fee35c125f4d79af0ad3569bcf91e3f` (the preceding documentation checkpoint).
- **Current code/test/workflow checkpoint:** `e1b18baf9962562bb363258586df5e710c54933f`. Commit chain: policy helper `cdbf2e8351e383892d3cdc8185ad9c8a2e24182d`; host test `456f09952353503443945c8cf932efb32ad19209`; production change `45e15098c5016bf6e71831c477b8adf5db708ef7`; initial workflow contract `ff4be91261099d7069db5b5bef0279853eecb85e`; workflow newline-escaping correction and final exact code/test/workflow head `e1b18baf9962562bb363258586df5e710c54933f`.
- **Recovered task:** the prior handoff explicitly directed continuation of NVRAM/configuration persistence and commit-result propagation after the profile parser/save fix. Inspected `trunk/user/httpd/src/nvram_x.c`, its header, `trunk/user/shared/src/nvram_linux.c`, `trunk/user/httpd/src/web_ex.c` commit/restore call sites, relevant RC NVRAM callers and the HTTPD Actions workflow.
- **Confirmed P1 defect:** `do_restore_nv_cgi()` stopped the watchdog, ran `/usr/sbin/nvram restore`, ignored the return from `nvram_commit()`, then rebooted solely according to the restore command result. The shared NVRAM implementation returns a failure when `/dev/nvram` open or `NVRAM_IOCTL_COMMIT` fails. This could reboot with restored values only in volatile state and lose the imported configuration.
- **Implementation:** new `httpd_nvram_restore_policy.h` gates success/reboot on both restore and commit return values being exactly zero. `do_restore_nv_cgi()` now calls commit only after restore succeeds; if either phase fails it logs the appropriate error, restarts the watchdog that was stopped for the upload, forces the request into the existing UploadError path, and does not reboot. Successful restore plus successful commit preserves the prior reboot flow. The workflow contains the compile/run regression and source-order/trigger contract; a follow-up corrected the embedded Python newline escaping.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/httpd/src/web_ex.c`; new `trunk/user/httpd/src/httpd_nvram_restore_policy.h`; new `trunk/user/httpd/src/tests/httpd_nvram_restore_policy.c`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Validation:** local `cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror` compile/run PASS (`NVRAM restore durability policy: PASS`); live-source structural verification PASS confirmed restore → commit → gate → watchdog recovery → reboot ordering, one commit invocation, both push/PR trigger entries, and corrected embedded-Python newline escapes.
- **Exact-head Actions:** **PENDING / NOT VERIFIED** for exact `head_sha=e1b18baf9962562bb363258586df5e710c54933f`. The combined-status endpoint returned no statuses and the available workflow-run connector only exposes PR-triggered runs; no run ID/URL or SUCCESS is claimed. Previous run [42472927950](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42472927950) passed only on prior profile-I/O code SHA `2c608709dcf7cd2f74f454f2cdae8a47af107a46`.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 source review and L2 host policy regression PASS. Full HTTPD production compile, MIPS/uClibc target compile, firmware/TRX, router profile restore and L4 hardware validation NOT RUN.
- **Residual risks / module status:** NVRAM/configuration persistence remains **AUDITING**, not complete. Restore replay can leave partial volatile configuration if a per-key SET fails after clear; commit failure now prevents reboot but does not roll back the already replayed in-memory values, and the uploaded profile is unlinked on error. Profile saving still writes directly to its destination (not atomic). `nvram_x.c::nvram_del_list_map_x()` ignores every set/unset result; its caller unconditionally publishes the reduced row count. `asus_nvram_commit()` clears `nvram_modified` before calling `nvram_commit_safe()`, while `nvram_commit_safe()` ignores commit failure; RC default restore, erase and startup commit callers also need propagation review. Temporary-vs-persistent semantics and migration remain open.
- **Remote persistence / governance:** no local-only changes. Branch inventory shows only `main`; open PR search returned none. Prior documentation refresh commits before this final correction: roadmap `fadf87f0c85d6e5fefd8d1d670ab0e8b1037d4fa`, dev log `11596ce4cbb8424a73b42a875f3955753a14078b`, handoff `6347e7ade001c17b416ce688978b5e436c816e30`. Latest roadmap refresh: `b046a01b228a87547f5cd84a5f7b377f8607415a`; latest dev-log refresh: `7ec6a8f067d53bf967561bcdaa4dd4e3a06bcdc7`. This handoff is the final documentation update in the current sequence; refetch live `main` before any next write.
- **Exact next task:** audit `nvram_del_list_map_x()` and `nvram_remove_group_item()` in `trunk/user/httpd/src/{nvram_x.c,web_ex.c}`. Trace the row compaction across every column and check all `nvram_set()`, `nvram_set_temp()` and `nvram_unset()` results. Design a bounded failure/recovery strategy so the group-count key is not reduced when one or more column rewrites failed; add a GNU89 host regression and workflow source-contract coverage. Do not claim NVRAM persistence is closed. Continue broad stability-first userland rotation and parallel security review after this atomic task; full MIPS/firmware and hardware validation remain later-stage milestones.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Fail closed on NVRAM profile I/O (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Fetch the live `main` HEAD before every subsequent write. Required conversation title remains `Padavan4.4接力开发标题一定要长才好删`.
- **Base HEAD:** `de3b3486f5e2c49b747ff7e8a247f3a7daedd7c4`.
- **Code / regression / workflow commit:** `2c608709dcf7cd2f74f454f2cdae8a47af107a46` — `fix(nvram): reject incomplete profile restores`.
- **Recovered task:** the prior checkpoint directed the project to rotate out of the completed ejected-storage fix and inspect NVRAM/configuration persistence. Reviewed `trunk/user/nvram/src/nvram.c`, its package and shared-library build integration, `trunk/user/shared/src/nvram_linux.c`, the NVRAM ioctl contract in `trunk/linux-4.4.x/drivers/nvram/nvram_linux.c` and `trunk/linux-4.4.x/include/nvram/bcmnvram.h`, plus `trunk/user/httpd/src/nvram_x.c`.
- **Confirmed defect:** `nvram_save()` ignored the NVRAM get-all result and every profile write/close result, so a failed backend read could be reported as a successful empty backup and short/disk-full writes could be reported as success. `nvram_restore()` trusted an unaligned HDR1 length field and read it into an `NVRAM_SPACE` allocation without a bound; HDR2 silently clamped its declared length and did not require a complete read/close. It cleared live NVRAM before validating the complete payload and ignored clear/set failures. A truncated or malformed profile could therefore damage live configuration or be reported as restored.
- **Implementation:** added `nvram_profile_policy.h` and a GNU89 host regression. HDR1's 32-bit little-endian length is decoded bytewise and capped at `NVRAM_SPACE`; HDR2's 24-bit length is capped at `NVRAM_SPACE + 1024` to preserve the existing save format's 1 KiB block-rounding behavior. Zero/unknown/oversized headers are rejected. The decoded payload must be a complete NUL-delimited `name=value` list, with nonempty names, parameter/value lengths within the kernel ioctl limits (63/4095), a final empty-record terminator and zero-only padding. Restore requires the exact declared byte count, no trailing data, no stream error and successful close, then validates the entire payload before calling `nvram_clear()`; clear and per-variable set failures are now propagated. Save checks `nvram_getall()` and payload validity before opening/truncating the destination, checks all writes and `fclose()`, and emits the format-compatible padding sentinel in a single bounded write instead of repeatedly reopening `/dev/urandom`. The random-byte read is initialized and checked.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/nvram/src/nvram.c`; new `trunk/user/nvram/src/nvram_profile_policy.h`; new `trunk/user/nvram/src/tests/nvram_profile_policy.c`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Regression / exact-head Actions:** local `cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror` compile and execution passed (`NVRAM profile policy: PASS`). GitHub Actions run [42472927950](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42472927950) completed **SUCCESS** on exact `head_sha=2c608709dcf7cd2f74f454f2cdae8a47af107a46`. Both `Compile and run policy regression` and the new `Verify NVRAM profile I/O fails closed before mutation` source-contract step passed; all 28 job steps completed successfully.
- **Validation boundary:** L1 source review and L2 GNU89 host regression / exact-head Actions PASS. No production MIPS/uClibc compile, complete firmware/TRX build, router runtime, backup/restore exercise on target or L4 hardware validation was performed.
- **Residual risks / audit state:** NVRAM/configuration persistence remains **AUDITING**, not complete. If `nvram_set()` fails after `nvram_clear()`, restore now reports failure but may still leave cleared/partially replayed configuration; transactional rollback is not implemented. Save writes directly to its destination rather than an atomic temporary-file/rename sequence, so a reported write failure can leave a partial backup. `nvram_x.c` list compaction and all RC/HTTPD write/commit callers have not been fully audited; check return propagation, temporary-vs-persistent semantics and migration paths next. Storage/hotplug also remains AUDITING.
- **Remote persistence:** code, tests and workflow are committed to `main` in `2c608709dcf7cd2f74f454f2cdae8a47af107a46`. The handoff, roadmap and development log were committed as documentation follow-up `69fcd2859fee35c125f4d79af0ad3569bcf91e3f`. No local-only patch is carried. The next conversation must refetch live `main` before writing; previous branch audit found only `main` and no open PRs.
- **Exact next task:** continue NVRAM/configuration persistence review at `trunk/user/httpd/src/nvram_x.c` and the RC write/commit callers. Trace `nvram_set`, `nvram_unset`, `nvram_clear`, `nvram_commit`, profile save/restore and temporary NVRAM paths through every caller. Prioritize one concrete partial-update or lost-commit stability defect, add host regression and workflow coverage, then commit it. Do not claim the NVRAM module is closed. Continue rotating across the whole userland with security in parallel; full MIPS/firmware and hardware validation remain consolidated later-stage milestones.

## Latest resumable checkpoint — Safe swap detection and hot-unplug handling (2026-11-08)

- **Active repository / branch:** `Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2`, `main`. Continue to fetch the live `main` HEAD before every new write.
- **Code / regression / Actions commit:** `f2c6c909a69709a21699eb2498153351f1227e34` — `fix(storage): distinguish active and unknown swap state`; parent/base `f788c64b5fedb83e9f0ed44d8ac4d518988a41a7`.
- **Recovered unfinished task:** the previous handoff explicitly left `try_device_swapoff()` in `trunk/user/libdisk/src/disk_initial.c` and its two ignored hotplug callers in `trunk/user/rc/src/hotplug_stor.c` for follow-up. The original `is_device_swapon()` collapsed open/header/read/close failures into “inactive”, and `try_device_swapoff()` invoked `swapoff()` even when no matching active swap existed.
- **Implemented:** introduced `storage_swap_policy.h` with bounded /dev path construction, strict `/proc/swaps` header and first-field parsing, exact device matching and tri-state results (inactive / active / unknown). Inventory I/O, malformed/truncated input and close failures now remain unknown. `create_partition()` propagates unknown state and `read_disk_data()` refuses to return a partial storage topology. `try_device_swapoff()` is a no-op for known-inactive devices, calls `swapoff()` only for known-active devices, and rechecks after a failed syscall to handle a concurrent successful disable. Global swap cleanup validates its inventory and reports read/close errors. Both SCSI and MMC unplug handlers log swapoff uncertainty/failure while preserving the existing unplug notification path.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/libdisk/src/disk_initial.c`, `trunk/user/libdisk/src/disk_initial.h`, new `trunk/user/libdisk/src/storage_swap_policy.h`, `trunk/user/rc/src/hotplug_stor.c`, new `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/storage_swap_policy.c`, `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`.
- **Validation:** host-side GNU89 policy regression compiled with `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` and passed (`Storage swap inventory policy: PASS`). GitHub Actions run [42469432175](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42469432175) completed **SUCCESS** on the exact `head_sha` of the code commit above. The job completed successfully, including both `Compile and run policy regression` and the new `Verify storage swap inventory distinguishes inactive, active and unknown` step. This is host/source CI evidence only. No firmware build, target cross-compile, TRX build or hardware test was performed.
- **Next immediate task:** continue the storage audit at `trunk/user/rc/src/services_stor.c::umount_ejected()`. It already checks `ferror()` for `/proc/mounts` and nested `/proc/partitions` reads, but currently ignores `fclose()` failures for those streams. Review long/truncated rows and close-error propagation; add an isolated host-testable policy/regression and workflow contract. Keep eject cleanup conservative when either inventory cannot be read completely.
- **Scope boundary:** storage/hotplug remains an open audit, not “complete”. After the next storage item, rotate to other userland subsystems according to the repository-wide governance ledger; do not camp on HTTPD or storage alone. Full firmware build and physical-router test remain deferred to the project-wide integration stage.

## Development direction update — 2026-11-08

- **Canonical repository handover:** active repository is now [Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2), default branch `main`. GitHub fork metadata reports parent `xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2` and source `tsl0922/padavan`. Use the Arlene206 URL for all new development, Actions, branches, releases, and clone instructions.
- **Historical references:** old xiaobei206 Actions URLs, commit records, and checkpoint links in this handoff are retained as historical evidence. Do not rewrite them or treat them as the active remote.
- **Toolchain handover:** the maintained repository already publishes both `mipsel-linux-musl.tar.xz` and `mipsel-linux-uclibc.tar.xz` in its [toolchain release](https://github.com/Arlene206/padavan-4.4-2/releases/tag/toolchain). The root Makefile and custom-build workflow now use these fork-owned assets.
- **Base HEAD before this maintenance update:** `7f7d3ea41ebdd7eeab8a303f3d4ad8dddb54a8a7`. Fetch live `main` before any subsequent write.

- Primary direction: find and fix stability bugs across the whole integrated Padavan user space. Continue security review in parallel; critical security vulnerabilities, data corruption, boot blockers and severe system failures remain immediate priorities.
- Preserve the existing GPT handoff workflow and historical checkpoints. Add newer resumable checkpoints without rewriting the project's documentation architecture.
- **Active branch:** `main`. **Code/test/workflow checkpoint before this documentation refresh:** `2f4828c42a1a0a449f92aa2f9f5fd3ab6dbdaa09` (`fix(storage): use portable automount lock descriptors`), based on documentation baseline `84013d94812460b66e92c611686722e2320497a3`. This documentation refresh is committed as a follow-on to that code checkpoint; the next conversation must fetch live `main` HEAD before any new write.
- **Recovered CI failure:** run [42460044825](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42460044825) failed on exact SHA `97c91cff20070c451a04f6c7fff4a3fa496d6588` at step 14, `Verify filesystem checker statuses gate read-write automount`. The concrete failure was `automount.sh: 15: Syntax error: word unexpected (expecting "}")`: Ubuntu's `dash` rejected the high-numbered file-descriptor redirection `500>/var/lock/fs_module.lock`. Runs [42458961923](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42458961923) and [42460028622](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42460028622) had the same underlying shell syntax failure. These are real CI failures, not external infrastructure failures.
- **Fix and exact-head Actions:** commit `2f4828c42a1a0a449f92aa2f9f5fd3ab6dbdaa09` changes the nested filesystem-module lock to fd 9 and the global mountpoint ownership lock to fd 8, keeping them separate because the module loader executes inside the outer critical section. Mount helpers close fd 8; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml` now asserts the low-numbered descriptors and rejects fd 500. Exact-head Actions [run 42460430403](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42460430403) completed **SUCCESS** on that exact SHA; job `128668345502` completed all 25 steps, including storage inventory and filesystem-checker/mount-gate checks.
- **Validation boundary:** local `dash -n` reproduced the previous fd-500 syntax failure (exit 2) and passed the fd-8/fd-9 replacement syntax. The exact-head workflow also passed. This is shell/source CI evidence only: MIPS/uClibc compilation, complete firmware/TRX build, target BusyBox `flock`, router runtime and physical hardware remain NOT RUN.
- **Remote checkpoint audit:** retain the historical divergent branches; do not merge stale trees wholesale. The last branch audit recorded no open PRs and did not identify a current unique checkpoint requiring merge. Refresh live refs before future branch mutations.
- **Whole-userland rotation remains mandatory:** storage/hotplug is still P1/P2 **AUDITING**, not closed. Firewall IPv6/standalone writers, WAN reconnect/watchdog/backoff, DHCP/DNS/routing, HTTPD/auth/upload/restore, configuration persistence and boot/system management remain in the global risk ledger.

## Previous resumable checkpoint — Repair POSIX shell descriptor portability in automount

- **Current source/test/workflow SHA:** `2f4828c42a1a0a449f92aa2f9f5fd3ab6dbdaa09`; active branch `main`.
- **Reviewed files and finding:** `trunk/user/scripts/files/sbin/automount.sh` used fd 500 for both `fs_module.lock` and `automount_mountpoint.lock`. The source-level lock design was sound in intent, but fd 500 redirection is not parsed by Ubuntu's POSIX `sh`/dash, so the workflow's required `sh -n` rejected the script before the host regression could complete.
- **Changed paths:** `trunk/user/scripts/files/sbin/automount.sh`; `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`. The nested module lock uses fd 9, and the outer mountpoint lock uses fd 8. All external mount helpers explicitly close fd 8 so children cannot accidentally prolong the mountpoint lock. Workflow assertions were updated to the actual descriptor layout.
- **Tests/CI:** local dash syntax check for the replacement descriptors PASS; original fd-500 syntax was reproduced as FAIL/exit 2. Exact-head [Actions run 42460430403](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42460430403) SUCCESS on `2f4828c42a1a0a449f92aa2f9f5fd3ab6dbdaa09`, 25/25 job steps. No target or hardware validation.
- **Next action at that historical checkpoint (completed above):** trace and repair `try_device_swapoff()` and its ignored SCSI/MMC hotplug callers. This follow-up was completed in code commit `f2c6c909a69709a21699eb2498153351f1227e34` and passed exact-head Actions run `42469432175`. Do not repeat it; the current next task is `services_stor.c::umount_ejected()` as recorded in the latest checkpoint at the top of this file.
- **Working state:** the fd portability fix is committed to `main`; exact-head Actions passed. No local-only source patch or pending checkpoint branch is carried. Do not claim the storage subsystem is fully audited, or claim target/hardware success.
