- **Finding / scope:** `start_firewall_ex()` obtains runtime WAN/MAN interface names and interpolates them into bare `-i`/`-o` tokens across generated IPv4/IPv6 rule tables. Before this change, there was no shared interface-token allowlist at that generation boundary. This is a token-integrity hardening gap; this round did not establish a complete remote attacker-controlled dataflow.
- **Fix:** `firewall_filter_policy.h::firewall_interface_name_is_safe()` accepts 1–15 ASCII interface-name characters, requiring an alphanumeric first byte and allowing only alphanumeric, underscore, dot, colon or hyphen after it; a single trailing `+` remains supported for iptables interface-prefix matching. `firewall_ex.c::start_firewall_ex()` validates nonempty MAN and configured WAN values before copying, validates the WAN fallback and built-in LAN name before any rule-table writer runs, and requires a safe MAN token when the MAN interface will be used. Invalid input is logged without echoing the raw token, and the firewall rebuild is refused before table generation.
- **Regression / CI:** `tests/firewall_filter_policy.c` covers common router interface names, PPP and wireless names, aliases, a trailing wildcard, null/empty/overlong strings, leading option-like characters, spaces, separators and a CR/LF-style restore-line payload. `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml` checks that source guards precede rule generation. Exact-head run 42419220664 compiled and ran the GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` policy regression and passed all existing source-contract and other regression steps. Workflow push/PR path sets were checked for equivalent coverage of the changed code/test paths.
- **Limits / still open:** L1 source review and focused L2 host regression/source contracts PASS. This does not complete the firewall module: alternative add-on restore inputs, remaining interface/NVRAM provenance, restore-failure recovery, safe cross-table rollback and independent IPv4/IPv6 semantic parity remain open. Production RC/MIPS build, firmware/TRX validation, live `iptables-restore` testing and physical-router testing were **NOT RUN**.
- **Remote checkpoint branches compared with this tested code SHA:** `refactor/bstream-tag-wire` 1 ahead / 566 behind; `refactor/dbapi-result-20261105` 8 / 647; `refactor/dynlib-lifecycle` 0 / 570; `refactor/sorting-boundaries` 0 / 566; `refactor/uarray-character-ctype` 2 / 564. Ahead-only deltas remain SkipDBv2 source/CMake/test/workflow/dev-log work; none was merged.
- **Next bounded task:** rotate out of firewall into the P1/P2 FTP/Samba/file-sharing attack surface (including HTTPD Aidisk/share-management routes), audit authentication, path normalization, permissions, privileged shell invocations and lifecycle, then continue USB/storage/hotplug and init/service management. Return to firewall rollback and IPv6 parity as separate open tasks. Do not return to routine SkipDBv2-only expansion.

## Previous resumable checkpoint — HTTPD privileged upload and storage-restore staging (exact-head Actions PASS)

- **Source/test/workflow verification SHA:** `f39ec03b4b9c898151048f4244276136d2c1fe33` on `main`; exact-head Actions run [`42417878853`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42417878853) completed **SUCCESS**. The current source changes span implementation commits `226f20b880a557004d1e55a20d37717517fb8aa1` and `7211a6be9067751bae7a94f30c5c4d6af93a938d`, then the CI source-contract correction `515223ee4f0c9178662e06b67a6bd1879fd47a9a` and shell syntax regression `f39ec03b4b9c898151048f4244276136d2c1fe33`.
- **Finding:** `trunk/user/httpd/src/upload.c::do_upload_file()` removed a predictable output and subsequently used `fopen(fn, "w+")`. In a shared writable temporary directory, a competing local process could race in a symlink and redirect a root HTTPD upload write. The fixed firmware image, router settings and storage archive paths also lived under shared `/tmp`; storage restore used a predictable shared scratch directory.
- **Fix:** new `trunk/user/httpd/src/httpd_upload_file.h` uses `open(O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_NOFOLLOW)`, forces mode 0600 with `fchmod`, and wraps the already-open descriptor with `fdopen`. The uploader uses it instead of `fopen`. Fixed firmware/settings/storage staging paths now live in `/tmp/.httpd-private/`. `dev_init.sh` sets `/tmp` to sticky mode 1777 and creates the staging directory mode 0700 before the storage startup load. Storage restore creates a unique mode-0700 scratch directory with a bounded `mkdir` loop, quotes paths, fails closed if no scratch slot is available, and copies the extracted contents into `/etc/storage` without relying on a fixed basename. This avoids moving firmware staging into the router's much smaller 1 MiB `/home` tmpfs.
- **Regression coverage:** new `trunk/user/httpd/src/tests/httpd_upload_file.c` creates a victim file plus target symlink, confirms exclusive creation rejects it without changing the victim, then verifies normal creation is a regular mode-0600 file. GitHub Actions compiles and runs this test under GNU89 `-Wall -Wextra -Werror`, checks source wiring and equal push/PR path filters, and runs `sh -n` on `mtd_storage.sh` and `dev_init.sh`. Exact-head run 42417878853 passed all job steps, including these checks.
- **CI debugging trail:** runs `42417600632` and `42417688730` failed during an added source-contract script because the test referenced `restore` before defining its source slice (Python `NameError`). Commit `515223ee4f0c9178662e06b67a6bd1879fd47a9a` moves the restore-slice definition ahead of the assertions; exact-head run `42417796130` passed. The follow-up shell-syntax check commit `f39ec03b4b9c898151048f4244276136d2c1fe33` then passed exact-head run `42417878853`.
- **Validation boundary:** this is a bounded host-side security/source-contract and shell-syntax regression, not a full target compile. Production MIPS build, firmware/TRX validation and physical-router testing were **NOT RUN**. HTTPD/TLS/runtime behavior, archive semantics and alternate writers are still under audit; FTP/Samba, USB/storage/hotplug and full init/service lifecycle remain open.
- **Checkpoint branches:** the five extant `refactor/*` branches remain hundreds of commits behind main, and their ahead-only deltas are SkipDBv2/CMake/test/workflow/dev-log work. None was merged.
- **Next bounded task:** continue the repository-wide P1 audit outside DHCP/HTTPD: review FTP/Samba sharing and path permissions or the remaining firewall restore/error/IPv6 policy gaps, then continue USB/storage/hotplug and init/service lifecycle. Do not treat this HTTPD fix as closing the full module.

## Previous resumable checkpoint — PPP secret-file hardening + DNSMasq input validation (exact-head host Actions PASS)

- **Current main verification head:** `73f921259959dbdf377943f46cb567051c83ef7c` (`test(ppp): align private mode source assertion`), following code commit `bd7d7fbbd69156cdd563579aaa85ee8762824719` (`fix(ppp): secure credential options before writing`). Exact-head Actions run [`42415669510`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42415669510) completed **SUCCESS** against `73f921259959dbdf377943f46cb567051c83ef7c`; every job step passed.
- **PPP finding:** `launch_wan_pppd()` wrote PPP configuration containing clear-text credentials with `fopen(options, "w")`, then applied `chmod(0600)` only after writing. The new `ppp_options_file.h` verifies/repairs `/tmp/ppp` directory permissions before use; rejects symlink, non-regular, wrong-owner, and multiply-linked options files; applies owner-only mode before truncation/writing using `O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK`, checks the opened inode, and checks flush/close. On finalization failure it removes the partial credentials file and aborts starting PPP.
- **PPP regression:** `tests/ppp_options_file.c` covers tightening an old 0644 options file before writing, final 0600 mode, directory mode, symlink/hardlink/FIFO rejection and victim preservation, plus forced flush/close failure cleanup. Workflow assertion was corrected in `73f9212599...`; exact-head run `42415669510` then passed all steps, including PPP, DNSMasq, firewall, root-managed script and HTTPD route/auth checks.
- **DNSMasq additions now included in current main:** `dnsmasq_file.h` and `services_ex.c` stage `/tmp/hosts.static` and `/tmp/dnsmasq.servers` with unique private temporary files, checked close/flush and atomic rename; lease creation rejects unsafe inode types/ownership/link count; WAN/startup paths reject untrusted snapshots and preserve a safe last-known-good server file; `dnsmasq_valid_server_domain()` validates the DHCP/VPN domain inserted in generated `server=/%s/%s` lines, rejecting whitespace, slashes and CR/LF-style directive injection. Tests cover actual CR/LF escape and slash payloads. Exact-head DNS/domain run [`42415272701`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42415272701) completed SUCCESS on `c81e0742a031e8fd722da522cb438d5b69f85e3c`.

## Previous resumable checkpoint — DNSMasq runtime file staging and lease-path validation (exact-head host Actions PASS)

- **Latest code checkpoint:** `c59a43645cca746cb7c8c6496b55ff4324ae5870`, on `main`. Previous bounded DNSMasq code milestones: `b3d9a48663ede904114c8b7587ea250aa49095b9`, `b503f6a7bea848d314335dc5a8a2b5791ba986ea`, `e21332e41652794a47963e79cd714aa97fb58a06`, and `314a9b31df790d70b99be98334c26a46109f878f`.
- **Finding:** privileged DNSMasq setup wrote predictable `/tmp/hosts.static` and `/tmp/dnsmasq.servers` directly; the former is later ingested into `/etc/hosts`. Lease-file initialization also opened `/tmp/dnsmasq.leases` without rejecting symlinks/non-regular files/hardlinks. WAN resolver refresh ignored DNS server snapshot failures and could ask dnsmasq to reload without a validated snapshot.
- **Implementation:** `trunk/user/rc/src/dnsmasq_file.h` adds unique `mkstemp` staging, flush/close/error preservation, permission normalization and atomic `rename`; `dnsmasq_file_is_safe_regular()` rejects non-regular, wrong-owner, multiply linked and group/world-writable server/host snapshots. Lease initialization uses `O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK`, validates inode type/owner/link count and preserves stricter existing read permissions while ensuring owner read/write.
- **Call sites:** `services_ex.c` stages/publishes static hosts and DNS server snapshots, validates lease initialization and refuses dnsmasq startup when snapshot refresh fails and there is no safe last-known-good server file. `net_wan.c` does not ingest an unsafe static-host file and blocks SIGHUP/reload if the DNS server snapshot cannot be validated. Existing safe server snapshots remain available as last-known-good state when a refresh fails.
- **Regression:** `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/dnsmasq_file.c`, run by the added workflow step `Verify DNSMasq generated file trust boundaries`, covers atomic replacement of a planted symlink, final/staging modes, finalization failure + cleanup, lease symlink/hardlink/FIFO rejection, target preservation and restrictive lease permissions. The source contract checks DNS/WAN fail-closed wiring.
- **Actions status:** run [`42414820481`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42414820481) passed on `b503f6a7bea848d314335dc5a8a2b5791ba986ea`; run [`42414860072`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42414860072) passed on `e21332e41652794a47963e79cd714aa97fb58a06`. Exact-head run [`42414909753`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42414909753) **FAILED** on `314a9b31df790d70b99be98334c26a46109f878f`: `-Werror=maybe-uninitialized` identified an uninitialized lease-mode argument passed to `open()`. Commit `c59a43645cca746cb7c8c6496b55ff4324ae5870` fixes that call to pass explicit creation mode constants, then derives normalized mode only after `fstat()`. New exact-head run [`42414963649`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42414963649) completed **SUCCESS** on `c59a43645cca746cb7c8c6496b55ff4324ae5870`; the corrected lease-mode helper compiled with `-Werror`, the DNSMasq regression passed, and all remaining HTTPD/firewall source/test steps also passed.
- **Verification boundary:** earlier DNSMasq versions passed host regression CI. At this newest checkpoint source was re-fetched and static wiring checks passed, but the final host run remains PENDING. MIPS target build, firmware image validation and router hardware tests are NOT RUN.
- **Next exact action:** DNSMasq host regressions are now verified. Continue a bounded WAN/PPP/service lifecycle review, starting with DHCP client event validation and resolver/route update ordering. Keep the prior firewall multi-table rollback design explicitly unresolved; do not pivot back to routine SkipDBv2-only work.

## Previous resumable checkpoint — Root extension hooks validated before privileged dispatch (exact-head host CI PASS)

- **Verified source/test/workflow checkpoint before this documentation update:** `dd9326876226ebf7948710a66a38ee883d5fb576`. The production implementation landed in `1010cef088825972272039103fc7ccafa7467ade` and its interpreter/execute-mode compatibility refinement in `6a0a196b0ec7423b6c3108f102c6e809f74892d4`; source-contract corrections were made in `e934796333ccf184b1bc3e0c1479abc065fb5bff` and finalized at the verified SHA above. The governance-record commit following this checkpoint is documentation-only; re-fetch actual `main` before any further change.
- **Exact-head CI:** GitHub Actions run [`42412978452`](https://github.com/xiaobei206/padavan-4.4-2/actions/runs/42412978452) completed SUCCESS on `dd9326876226ebf7948710a66a38ee883d5fb576`; job `128524878720` passed every step, including GNU89 host regressions, AP migration, add-on firewall-hook staging, command-status reporting, root-managed executable staging, and HTTPD auth/route-access checks.
- **Finding and fix:** `start_firewall_ex()` previously dispatched `/etc/storage/post_iptables_script.sh` and `/opt/bin/update_iptables.sh` directly after a pathname existence check. These are privileged extension hooks with different provenance (persistent storage writable through the trusted HTTPD script writer; externally managed Optware storage), and dispatch did not independently enforce the source file's inode/type/link/mode contract. Both hooks now use `firewall_hook_file_stage()` before execution. It opens without following symlinks and nonblocking, verifies the opened inode is a regular file owned by the effective UID, single-linked, not group/other-writable, and at most 1 MiB; validates the private staging directory; copies from the opened descriptor to a unique private file under `/home/root`; runs only that staged file; and removes it afterward. Missing optional hooks are ignored; other validation/staging errors are logged and the hook is not run.
- **Compatibility detail:** the staging helper uses mode `0600` for non-executable inputs, and `0700` where the source owner-execute bit is present, preserving the script's shebang/interpreter without copying group/other permissions. The Optware hook's `update` argument is preserved. The helper remains shared with the optional Shadowsocks rule hook.
- **Host regression coverage:** `trunk/user/rc/src/tests/firewall_hook_file.c` is compiled with `cc -std=gnu89 -Wall -Wextra -Werror` and executed in the passing workflow. Cases cover normal staging and cleanup, executable source -> owner-only `0700`, symlink, group/other writable modes, hardlink, FIFO, unsafe staging-directory mode, and over-limit size. Workflow source contracts cover the two staged dispatches, log-on-rejection behavior, and hook cleanup. Two intermediate Actions attempts failed because their source-contract expectations were stale or incorrectly escaped; the final exact-head run above is authoritative.
- **Verification levels:** L1 static source review PASS; L2 focused host regression PASS on the exact source/test/workflow SHA. Production `rc` compilation, MIPS target build, firmware artifact validation, actual firewall restore, and router hardware behavior are NOT RUN / NOT VERIFIED.

The HTTPD variable row uses `EVM_RESTART_FIREWALL|EVM_BLOCK_UNSAFE`; the `scripts.*` writer maps it to `STORAGE_SCRIPTS_DIR`, requires a trusted session for `.sh` writes, and chmods the script 0755. Dispatch executes the path directly (no arguments); return failures are now logged. The dispatch does not independently lstat/validate owner, type, link count or mode before execution, so this remains an administrator-controlled root extension whose storage restore and runtime trust boundary must still be assessed.
- Optware hook inventory: `opt-mount.sh` creates `/opt/bin/update_iptables.sh` only if missing, chmods that default 0755 and links it as `/opt/etc/init.d/S10iptables`; `/opt` is bind-mounted from the configured Optware storage tree, and `opt-start.sh` runs executable `S??*` entries. Firewall restart invokes the hook with `update`. Existing files are not normalized or owner/mode-checked at dispatch, and the actual external filesystem owner/mode was not verified. Treat it as trusted admin-managed root extension code, but keep deployment provenance/mode enforcement open.

This closes the gap where an unclassified new route could previously be added without a failure. No production C route policy was changed because the reviewed current flags were correct.
- Session finding: the active-management lock uses the HTTPD-observed source address, not a distinct per-browser session. Protected routes still call auth_check() before executing their input/output callback. Logout.asp is intentionally exempted so its page can run; it sends HEAD /logout to trigger the browser Basic-auth cache reset challenge. http_logout() only clears the lock when the caller address equals login_ip. A same-observed-source client (for example, clients collapsed behind upstream NAT/proxy/SNAT) can force lock reset/re-authentication through this logout path or the failed-auth reset path. This is forced logout/availability impact, not a way to access protected routes without valid Basic credentials. No cookie/session or Origin-token redesign was made in this bounded round; compatibility and scope require a separate decision.

All file classes still pass through the common filename validation and writer; `validate_cgi()` skips File entries rather than persisting them as NVRAM.
- Bounded consumer trace: `rc.c` starts `started_script.sh` and runs `shutdown_script.sh`; `net_wan.c` invokes `post_wan_script.sh` on WAN up/down and `inet_state_script.sh` for Internet-state changes; `firewall_ex.c` invokes `post_iptables_script.sh`; VPN server/client code invokes the configured up/down hooks; button handling invokes `ez_buttons_script.sh`; AP startup/auto-start invokes `ap_script.sh`. The other add-on-specific DDNS/FRP/Shadowsocks/Adbyby scripts are inventoried as registered files but their complete downstream consumer semantics are not yet traced.
- P1 local TOCTOU finding: `write_textarea_to_file()` previously removed a predictable `/tmp/.<filename>` and then called `fopen(path, "w")` through `write_file_dos2unix()`. A local process able to create entries in shared `/tmp` could race that unlink/open gap with a symlink and redirect the privileged HTTPD write. This is a static source finding; an exploit race was not stress-tested.
- Fix: split the CRLF writer into `write_file_dos2unix_fp()` plus the existing path wrapper; staging now uses `mkstemp("/tmp/httpd-upload-XXXXXX")` and `fdopen()` on the exact returned descriptor. The unique file is created atomically with restrictive 0600 permissions and is not reopened by pathname; the final destination writer retains its existing path behavior. Write/close failures are now reported from the stream helper and the staging path is unlinked on error and after comparison.
- Source/workflow files changed: `trunk/user/httpd/src/web_ex.c` and `.github/workflows/httpd-security.yml`. Workflow contracts now require the mkstemp → fdopen → descriptor-writer ordering and reject the former predictable `/tmp/.<filename>` template.
- Exact-head Actions: HTTPD Authentication and Security Regression run `42398764761` completed SUCCESS on exact source SHA `0bbd8062e8bf7842e4879032aca3c1781bd393fa`; job `128481780467` completed SUCCESS. The GNU89 policy regression compile/run step passed, as did production-source wiring assertions, including the new secure staging-file contract and the existing auth/session/CGI/firewall checks. This is focused host/structural verification, not a complete `web_ex.c` production compilation.
- Validation boundary: L1 source/call-path review PASS; L2 existing GNU89 policy regressions and exact-head Actions PASS. A concurrent symlink-race stress test, MIPS target build, XY-C1 firmware image inspection, live router behavior and L4 hardware validation were NOT RUN.

No checkpoint contains relevant HTTPD work; none was merged.
